{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{k}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\mid{\mathbf{w}}_{k}]\leq{\frac{3}{4}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{k}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

Applying the argument iteratively over
E
epochs, and the law of iterated expectations, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{[E]}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{E+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq(\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

(7)

Thus choosing
E=\lceil\log_{4/3}(\frac{\beta D^{2}}{2\epsilon})\rceil
, where
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq D
,
we have

E\geq\log_{4/3}(\frac{\beta\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}}{2\epsilon})~\implies~(\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{\beta}.

Thus from (
7
), we get:

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{[E]}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{E+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{\beta},

and further applying
\beta
-smoothness of
f
, we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{[E]}}[f({\mathbf{w}}_{E+1})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})]\leq{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{[E]}}[\frac{\beta}{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{E+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\epsilon,

which proves the correctness of
Algorithm

2
for the choice of total number of epochs
k_{\epsilon}=E
.

Proof of Sample Complexity.
In order to verify that
Algorithm

2
indeed converges to an
\epsilon
-optimal point in
O(\epsilon^{-2p})
sample complexity, note we simply need to count the total sample complexity incurred in the
k_{\epsilon}
epochwise runs of
Relative-Gradient-Descent
(see Line #5 of
Algorithm

2
). However, by design of
Epoch-RGD
(
Algorithm

2
), since
Relative-Gradient-Descent
\big({\mathbf{w}}_{k},\eta_{k},\gamma_{k},t_{k}\big)
is run for only
t_{k}=\frac{1}{2B^{2}{(D_{k}^{2})}^{2p}}
iterations, the total sample complexity of
Epoch-RGD
becomes:

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{k_{\epsilon}}t_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2B^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{k_{\epsilon}}\frac{1}{{(D_{k}^{2})}^{2p}}=\frac{1}{2B^{2}(D^{2})^{2p}}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{2p}}+\frac{1}{((\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{2p})^{2}}+\cdots+\frac{1}{((\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{2p})^{k_{\epsilon}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2B^{2}(D^{2})^{2p}}\frac{(\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}^{2p})^{k_{\epsilon}}-1}{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}^{2p}-1}\leq\frac{1}{4B^{2}(D^{2})^{2p}}\Big((\nicefrac{{\beta D^{2}}}{{2\epsilon}})^{2p}-1\Big)=O\Big(\frac{1}{B^{2}\epsilon^{2p}}\Big)

where the last inequality is since
k_{\epsilon}=\lceil\log_{4/3}(\frac{\beta D^{2}}{2\epsilon})\rceil
by definition.
Thus follows the claimed sample complexity of
Epoch-RGD
in
6
and this concludes the proof.
∎

5
Conclusion and Perspective

We consider the problem of convex optimization under a general class of pairwise preferences (dueling) feedback.
Note the primary difficulty towards designing an efficient algorithm for the purpose lies in the fact that we can not hope to estimate the gradient of
f
for any general dueling/pairwise preference model. Thus we can not apply the standard
gradient descent
based techniques to address this problem.
We get around with the difficulty by estimating a
p
-th order proxy of the gradient, called
p^{th}
-degree-scaled
Gradient
. The crux of the idea lies in designing
Relative-Gradient-Descent
based algorithm (
Algorithm

1
), which is a generalized notion of gradient descent based optimization technique. Using this we design an efficient algorithm with convergence rate of
\smash{\widetilde{O}}(\epsilon^{-4p})
for a smooth convex objective function, and an optimal rate of
\smash{\widetilde{O}}(\epsilon^{-2p})
when the objective is smooth and strongly convex.

Future work.

Although the derived convergence rate for the strongly convex setting is information theoretically tight, the exact convergence lower bound is unclear for the class of smooth functions, which might be an interesting problem to pursue independently. Another open problem is to analyze this problem beyond the smoothness assumption.
Considering a regret minimization objective instead of the optimization perspective, as well as understanding the information theoretic regret performance limit would be interesting direction as well.
One can also consider generalizing the optimization framework to subsetwise preferences, instead of just pairwise (dueling) feedback. It might also be useful to extend our setup for contextual scenarios, adversarial preferences or non-stationary function sequences and understand the scopes of feasible solutions as well as the impossibility results.

Supplementary:
Dueling Convex Optimization with General Preferences

Appendix A
Useful Results (used in
Sections

3
,
3.2
and
4.2
)

Lemma 8
.

For a given vector
{\mathbf{g}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
and a random unit vector
{\mathbf{u}}
drawn uniformly from
{\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1)
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]=\frac{\tilde{c}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}{\sqrt{d}},

for some universal constant
\tilde{c}\in[\tfrac{1}{20},1]
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality we can assume
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=1
, since one can divide by
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
in both side of Lem.
8
without affecting the claim.
Now to bound
{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]
, note that since
{\mathbf{u}}
is drawn uniformly from
{\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1)
, by rotation invariance this equals
{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]
.
For an upper bound, observe that by symmetry
{\mathbf{E}}[u_{1}^{2}]=\tfrac{1}{d}{\mathbf{E}}[\sum_{i=1}^{d}u_{i}^{2}]=\tfrac{1}{d}
and thus

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]\leq\sqrt{{\mathbf{E}}[u_{1}^{2}]}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}.

We turn to prove a lower bound on
{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]
.
If
{\mathbf{u}}
were a Gaussian random vector with i.i.d. entries
u_{i}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,1/d)
, then from standard properties of the (truncated) Gaussian distribution we would have gotten that
{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]=\sqrt{2/\pi d}
.
For
{\mathbf{u}}
uniformly distributed on the unit sphere,
u_{i}
is distributed as
v_{1}/\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
where
{\mathbf{v}}
is Gaussian with i.i.d. entries
{\mathcal{N}}(0,1/d)
.
We then can write

\displaystyle\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}}}\right)}}

\displaystyle=\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\geq\frac{\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}}}\right)}}\geq\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\mbox{ and }\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\right)}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}<\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}}\right)}}-\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\right)}}.

Since
\sqrt{d}v_{1}
is a standard Normal, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}<\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}}\right)}}=\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left({-1<\sqrt{d}v_{1}<1}\right)}}=2\Phi(1)-1\leq 0.7,

and since
{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]=1
an application of Markov’s inequality gives

\displaystyle\Pr\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\Pr\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}>\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\epsilon^{2}{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{v}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]=\epsilon^{2}.

For
\epsilon=\tfrac{1}{4}
this implies that
\Pr\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq 1/4\sqrt{d}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\tfrac{1}{5}
, whence

{\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]={\mathbf{E}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{u_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]\geq 1/20\sqrt{d}.

∎

Lemma 9
.

Let
g:{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\to{\mathbb{R}}
be differentiable and let
{\mathbf{u}}
be a random unit vector in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
.
Then

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}[g({\mathbf{u}}){\mathbf{u}}]=\frac{1}{d}{\mathbf{E}}[\nabla g(u)].

Proof.

The above claim follows from Lemma 1 of
Flaxman et al. 2005
which shows that for any differentiable function
f:{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\to{\mathbb{R}}
and any
{\mathbf{x}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}[f({\mathbf{x}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}){\mathbf{u}}]=\frac{\delta}{d}\nabla{\mathbf{E}}[f({\mathbf{x}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})]=\frac{\delta}{d}{\mathbf{E}}[\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})].

Fix
{\mathbf{x}}=0
and substitute
f({\mathbf{z}})=g(\tfrac{1}{\gamma}{\mathbf{z}})
.
Then
\nabla f({\mathbf{z}})=\frac{1}{\gamma}\nabla g(\frac{1}{\gamma}{\mathbf{z}})
, and we obtain:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}[g({\mathbf{u}}){\mathbf{u}}]=\frac{1}{d}{\mathbf{E}}[\nabla g({\mathbf{u}})].

∎

Lemma 10
.

Suppose
f:{\mathcal{D}}\mapsto{\mathbb{R}}
is a convex function for some convex set
{\mathcal{D}}\subseteq{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
such that for any
{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
,
f({\mathbf{x}})-f({\mathbf{y}})>\epsilon
. Then this implies
\|\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})\|>\frac{\epsilon}{\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{y}}\|}
.
Further, assuming
D:=\max_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{y}}\|_{2}
, we get
\|\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})\|>\frac{\epsilon}{D}
for any
{\mathbf{x}}\in{\mathcal{D}}
.

Proof.

The proof simply follows using convexity of
f
as:

\displaystyle f({\mathbf{x}})-f({\mathbf{y}})>\epsilon

\displaystyle\implies\epsilon<f({\mathbf{x}})-f({\mathbf{y}})\leq\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})({\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{y}})\leq\|\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{y}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\implies\|\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})\|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{y}}\|}.

∎

Appendix B
Appendix for
Section

3

Lemma 11

(Estimation of
p^{th}
-degree-scaled
Gradient
from Dueling Feedback from general transfer function
\rho
)
.

Consider any general admissible transfer function
\rho:{\mathbb{R}}\mapsto[-1,1]
(that satisfies
1
).
Then if
f:{\mathcal{D}}\mapsto{\mathbb{R}}
is
L
-Lipschitz and
\beta
-smooth, given any
{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathcal{D}}
such that
r\geq\frac{2L\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\beta\sqrt{d}}
,
o\sim\mathrm{Ber}^{\pm}\big(\rho\big(f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)\big)
where
{\mathbf{u}}\sim\text{Unif}({\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1))
, and suitably tuned step-size
\gamma>0
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}},o}

\displaystyle[o{\mathbf{u}}]\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\begin{cases}C(d,\beta,c_{\rho},p,r,L)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2p-1}\nabla{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),&\text{ for any }p\geq 1,\\
\frac{4\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}\nabla{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),&\text{ for }p=1.\end{cases}

Here
\nabla{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}})
denotes the gradient of the smoothed function
{\tilde{f}}
at
{\mathbf{w}}
, where
{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}}):={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\text{Unif}({\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1))}[f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})]
, and

C(d,\beta,c_{\rho},p,r,L)
is a constant dependent on the problem parameters
d,\beta,c_{\rho},p,r,L
.

B.1
Proof of
11

See
11

Proof.

We start by noting that given
{\mathbf{w}}
and denoting
{\mathbf{g}}=o{\mathbf{u}}
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}},o}[{\mathbf{g}}]

\displaystyle={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}[{\mathbf{E}}_{o}[o{\mathbf{u}}\mid{\mathbf{u}}]]

\displaystyle={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}[\rho\big(f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big){\mathbf{u}}]

Let us start with the case for
p\geq 1
. Note since
\rho
is differentiable by assumption (see
1
-i) as well as
f
, using
9
in the first equality, we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

(8)

where the second equality follows since
\rho^{\prime}(-x)=\rho^{\prime}(x)
by the anti-symmetry of
\rho
(see
1
-(i)).
Note in
Section

B.1
, we further lower bounded
\rho^{\prime}
by
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}
using
1
-(ii) along with the observation that
\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})
is positive for any
{\mathbf{u}}\in{\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1)
by first order optimality conditions.

It is important to note that, to ensure
r
-proximity to the origin, as needed in
1
-(ii), it needed to satisfy
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq r
, which we ensured by choosing
\gamma\leq\frac{r}{2L}
(as recall by assumption
f
is
L
-lipschitz).

Case:
p=1
.
Note in this case from
Section

B.1
we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{c_{\rho}\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{2\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{2\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}\big(\nabla{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\nabla{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})=\frac{4\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}{\nabla{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}})}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

where the inequality follows since when
p=1
,
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}(x)=c_{\rho}
(independent of
x,\,\forall x\in{\mathbb{R}}
). The last equality follows by exchanging expectation and derivative (since
f
is differentiable and finite valued by assumptions).

Case:
p\geq 1
.
Now lets consider the case for any general
p\geq 1
: Now for the first term in
Section

B.1
, again applying the
\beta
-smoothness of
f
we get:

\displaystyle\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}})\leq\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}})\leq-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}.

Subtracting the inequalities, we get

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\right\rvert}}\leq\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle 2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\beta\gamma^{2}\leq f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\leq 2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\beta\gamma^{2}.

Note above implies:

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\right\rvert}}\geq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

Now using Lem. 4 of
Saha et al. 2021b
, we know that:

{\mathbf{P}}_{\mathbf{u}}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\beta\gamma\big)\geq 1-\lambda,\text{ where }\lambda=\inf_{\gamma^{\prime}>0}\bigg\{\gamma^{\prime}+\frac{2\beta\gamma\sqrt{d\log(1/\gamma^{\prime})}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\bigg\}

for any
{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
. Note choosing
\gamma^{\prime}=\frac{\beta\gamma\sqrt{d}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}
, we get
\lambda\leq\frac{\beta\gamma\sqrt{d}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\bigg(1+2\sqrt{\log\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\sqrt{d}\beta\gamma}}\bigg)
. Note if we further choose
\gamma\leq\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{10\beta\sqrt{d}}
, we have
\lambda\leq 1/2
.

Thus we have:

{\mathbf{P}}_{\mathbf{u}}\big(\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\right\rvert}}\geq\beta\gamma^{2}\big)\geq 1-\lambda,

and using
Section

B.1
, we further get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma(1-\lambda)}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\big(\beta\gamma^{2}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

(9)

Here it is important to note that above lower bound holds since (a) from first order optimality conditions for any
{\mathbf{u}}\in{\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1)
,

f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})>0
, and also
(b)
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\big(x\big)>0
for any
x>0
by definition of
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}
.

Thus from
Section

B.1
we further get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma(1-\lambda){\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\big(\beta\gamma^{2}\big)}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{2c_{\rho}p(1-\lambda)\gamma^{2p-1}\beta^{p-1}}{d}\big(\nabla\tilde{f}({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})+\nabla\tilde{f}({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big)\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

(10)

The result now follows by noting
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}(x)=c_{\rho}px^{p-1}\text{ for any }x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
,
\nabla{\tilde{f}}({\mathbf{w}})={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\text{Unif}({\mathcal{S}}_{d}(1))}[\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})]
and choosing
\gamma=\min\Big(\frac{r}{2L},\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{10\beta\sqrt{d}}\Big)
. That concludes the proof for any
p\geq 1
. ∎

Remark 3
.

It is also worth noting that, for
p=0
,
c_{\rho}=1
, our transfer function recovers the
\sign
feedback of
Saha et al. 2021b
. In this case it can be shown that:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}},o}

\displaystyle[o{\mathbf{u}}]\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{40\sqrt{d}}\frac{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}},

which ssentially recovers the normalized gradient estimate (or the direction of the gradient).

The claim for
p=0
simply follows by consecutively applying Lemma
4
and
3
of
Saha et al. 2021b
as follows: From Lemma
4
of
Saha et al. 2021b
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle[\sign\big(f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big){\mathbf{u}}]\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq(1-\lambda){\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}[\sign\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}\big){\mathbf{u}}]\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

where
\lambda
is as defined in the Case for
p\geq 1
above. But using Lemma
3
of
Saha et al. 2021b
we further get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}}

\displaystyle[\sign\big(f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\big){\mathbf{u}}]\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{40\sqrt{d}}\frac{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}},

which concludes the claim choosing
\gamma=\min\Big(\frac{r}{2L},\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{10\beta\sqrt{d}}\Big)
(noting that
\lambda\leq 1/2
for this choice of
\gamma
as explained in the case for
p\geq 1
above).

Appendix C
Appendix for
Section

3.2

Notations.
We denote by
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
the history
\{{\mathbf{w}}_{\tau},{\mathbf{u}}_{\tau},o_{\tau}\}_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\cup{\mathbf{w}}_{t}
till time
t
, for all
t\in[T]
.
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\cdot]:={\mathbf{E}}_{o_{t},{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\cdot\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}]
denote the expectation with respect to
{\mathbf{u}}_{t},o_{t}
given
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}

C.1
Proof of
4

See
4

Complete Proof of
4
.

First note by our update rule,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle={\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]-2{\eta}{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\eta^{2}{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]+\eta^{2}.

(11)

But since projection to
{\mathcal{D}}
reduces distance from
{\mathbf{w}}^{*}
we have:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}

This further implies:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\leq\gamma^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

Applying above in
Section

C.1
we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]+\eta^{2}.

(12)

On the other hand, since both
f
and
\rho
is convex (by assumption), using
9
we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\cdot{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big({f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\big)\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\big)\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}),

(13)

where the last equality follows since
\rho(-x)=-\rho(x)
(see
1
-i).
Now, since
f
is convex and
\beta
-smooth, we have that:

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}(\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})\boldsymbol{\cdot}(\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\beta\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})+\gamma(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*}))\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

Likewise, for the term
\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})
we get,

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}(\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})\boldsymbol{\cdot}(\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\beta\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\gamma(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*}))\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

Summing, we thus get:

\displaystyle\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\big)\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq 2(f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*}))-2\beta\gamma^{2}.

(14)

Now since by
1
-(2) we have
\rho^{\prime}(x)>0
for any
x\in(0,r]
, we can claim that

\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)>0;

see
4
for a formal justification (on choices of
\gamma
to satisfy
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq r
).

Remark 4
.

Recall from
1
, there exists some constants
p,r,c_{\rho}>0
such that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho(x)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}(x)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
forall

. Clearly we apply inequality
(a)
for
x=f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})
. Now to justify indeed
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq r
, we note that since
f
is
\beta
-smooth,
it is also locally-lipschitz inside the bounded domain
{\mathcal{D}}
and suppose
L
is the resulting lipschitz constant.
Then we have
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq 2L\gamma
,
and to ensure the above condition, we can assume
2L\gamma\leq r
by choosing small enough
\gamma
. But since we set
\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\epsilon}{\beta D\sqrt{d}}
, note the constraints are satisfied for any
\epsilon\leq\frac{r\beta D\sqrt{d}}{2L\tilde{c}}
.

For simplicity, let us denote
{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\cdot]:={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\cdot\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}]
.
Recall, since
\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)>0

, now using
