\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]=\frac{\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\big)\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle>\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\epsilon-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}~\big(\text{since we conditioned on }f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon\big),

\displaystyle>\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\epsilon/2},

(15)

where the second last inequality follows since we choose
\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\epsilon}{\beta\sqrt{d}D}
(recall from
3
). However since

, note we have

and further since

,
3
3

3

Note otherwise, i.e. if

, for any point

,

by

and the optimization problem of
3
becomes trivial to solve
above in turn implies

and hence
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\epsilon-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\frac{\epsilon}{2}
. Last equality is from
1
-(ii) where recall that
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}(x)=c_{\rho}\sign(x)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{x}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{p}
forall denotes the

.
Now applying the
\beta
-smoothness of

:

\displaystyle\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}})\leq\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}})\leq-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\beta\gamma^{2}.

Subtracting the inequalities, we get

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})}\right\rvert}}\leq\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle 2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\beta\gamma^{2}\leq f({\mathbf{w}}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}})-f({\mathbf{w}}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}})\leq 2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})+\beta\gamma^{2}.

Note above implies:

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}\geq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

Now taking expectation over
{\mathbf{u}}_{t}
in both side:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}]\geq{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]-\beta\gamma^{2}=2\frac{\tilde{c}\gamma\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\sqrt{d}}-\beta\gamma^{2}

where the last equality is due to
8
.
Additionally, since we assumed
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon
, i.e. the suboptimality gap to be at least
\epsilon
, by
10
we can further derive a lower bound:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}]\geq\frac{2\tilde{c}\gamma\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\beta\gamma^{2}={\frac{2\tilde{c}\gamma\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}D}}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

And now note that setting
\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\epsilon}{\beta\sqrt{d}D}
, the right hand side is positive. Now, lower bounding
\rho^{\prime}
by
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}
as per
1
-(ii) and further applying monotonicity of
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}(\cdot)
in the positive orthant, we get:

\displaystyle\rho^{\prime}({\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}])\geq\rho^{\prime}({\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}])\geq{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\bigg({\frac{2\tilde{c}\gamma\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}D}}-\beta\gamma^{2}\bigg).

(16)

Then from
Section

C.1
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\bigg({\frac{2\tilde{c}\gamma\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}D}}-\beta\gamma^{2}\bigg)\nicefrac{{\epsilon}}{{2}}~~(\text{applying the bound from from }\eqref{eq:may15a})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}\Big(c_{\rho}p\gamma^{p-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{2\tilde{c}\epsilon}{\sqrt{d}D}-\beta\gamma}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{p-1}\Big)\nicefrac{{\epsilon}}{{2}}~~(\text{as, }{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}(x)=c_{\rho}px^{p-1}\text{ for any }x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}),

(17)

Then for the above choice of
\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\epsilon}{\beta\sqrt{d}D}
, we finally get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma\epsilon}{2d}\big(c_{\rho}p\gamma^{p-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{\tilde{c}^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{{d}D^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{p-1}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{pc_{\rho}\tilde{c}^{2p-1}\epsilon^{2p}}{d^{(2p+1)/2}\beta^{p}D^{2p-1}},

(18)

Combining
Section

C.1
with
Section

C.1
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-2\eta\Bigg(\frac{pc_{\rho}\tilde{c}^{2p-1}\epsilon^{2p}}{d^{(2p+1)/2}\beta^{p}D^{2p-1}}\Bigg)+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\Bigg(\frac{pc_{\rho}\tilde{c}^{2p-1}\epsilon^{2p}}{d^{(2p+1)/2}\beta^{p}D^{2p-1}}\Bigg)^{2},~~\text{ setting }\eta=\Bigg(\frac{pc_{\rho}\tilde{c}^{2p-1}\epsilon^{2p}}{d^{(2p+1)/2}\beta^{p}D^{2p-1}}\Bigg),

which concludes the claim of
4
.
∎

C.2
Proof of
3

See
3

Complete Proof of
3
.

We denote by
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
the history
\{{\mathbf{w}}_{\tau},{\mathbf{u}}_{\tau},o_{\tau}\}_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\cup{\mathbf{w}}_{t}
till time
t
.

We start by noting that by definition:

{\mathbf{E}}_{o_{t}}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t},{\mathbf{u}}_{t}]=\rho(f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})){\mathbf{u}}_{t}

We proceed with the proof inductively, i.e. given
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
and assuming (conditioning on)
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon
, we can show that
{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}
always come closer to the minimum
{\mathbf{w}}^{*}
on expectation in terms of the
\ell_{2}
-norm. More formally, given
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
and assuming
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon
we will show:

{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}

where
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\cdot]:={\mathbf{E}}_{o_{t},{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\cdot\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}]
denote the expectation with respect to
{\mathbf{u}}_{t},o_{t}
given
{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
. The precise statement is given by
4
.

Given the statement of
4
, now note that by iteratively taking expectation over
{\mathcal{H}}_{T}
on both sides of
Equation

1
and summing over
t=1,\ldots,T
, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{p^{2}(\tilde{c}^{2p-1}c_{\rho})^{2}\epsilon^{4p}}{d^{2p+1}\beta^{2p}D^{4p-2}}T.

However, note if we set
T=\frac{d^{2p+1}\beta^{2p}D^{4p}}{p^{2}(\tilde{c}^{2p-1}c_{\rho})^{2}\epsilon^{4p}}
,
this implies

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq 0
, or equivalently
{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}={\mathbf{w}}^{*}
, which concludes the claim.

To clarify further, note we show that for any run of Alg.
Algorithm

1
if indeed
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon
continues to hold for all
t=1,2,\ldots T
, then
{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}={\mathbf{w}}^{*}
at
T=\frac{d^{2p+1}\beta^{2p}D^{4p}}{p^{2}(\tilde{c}^{2p-1}c_{\rho})^{2}\epsilon^{4p}}
. If not, there must have been a time
t\in[T]
such that
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})<\epsilon
. This concludes the proof with
T_{\epsilon}=T
.
∎

C.3
Proof of
5

See
5

Proof.

Case 1. Linear transfer functions:
\rho(x)=c_{\rho}x,~\forall x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
. So in this case
\rho
is the
p
-th order proxy  of itself, i.e.
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}=\rho
with
p=1
and any
r\in{\mathbb{R}}
.

Here
\rho^{\prime}(x)=c_{\rho}
for any
x\in{\mathbb{R}}
.
Then following the same steps as derived in the proof of
3
, note we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big({f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\gamma}{d}c_{\rho}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})-\beta\gamma^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Moreover, since we conditioned on
f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})>\epsilon
, plugging this in above and combining with

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{4\eta\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}\Bigg(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}-\beta\gamma^{2}\Bigg)+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{4\eta\gamma c_{\rho}}{d}\Bigg(\epsilon-\beta\gamma^{2}\Bigg)+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{4\eta c_{\rho}}{d}\big(\frac{\epsilon^{3/2}}{2\sqrt{2\beta}}\big)+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{c_{\rho}^{2}\epsilon^{3}}{2\beta d^{2}},

where
(a)
follows by setting
\gamma=\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon}{2\beta}}
, and
(b)
follows by setting
\eta=\frac{c_{\rho}\epsilon^{3/2}}{d\sqrt{2\beta}}
.

Then same as the proof of
3
, now iteratively taking expectations over
{\mathcal{H}}_{T}
on both sides of
Equation

1
and summing over
t=1,\ldots,T
, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{c_{\rho}^{2}\epsilon^{3}}{2\beta d^{2}}T.

However, note if we set
T=\frac{2d^{2}\beta D^{2}}{c_{\rho}^{2}\epsilon^{3}}
,
this implies

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq 0
, or equivalently
{\mathbf{w}}_{T+1}={\mathbf{w}}^{*}
, which concludes the claim (similarly as line of argument we concluded the proof of
3
).

Case 2. Sigmoid transfer functions:
\rho(x)=\frac{1-e^{-\omega x}}{1+e^{-\omega x}},~\forall x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
,
\omega>0
.
In this case the it can be shown that
\rho
can be approximated by a linear function near the origin, or more specifically, depending on the constant
\omega
, there exists
c_{\rho}^{\omega}
and
r^{\omega}
such that

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho(x)}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq c_{\rho}^{\omega}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{x}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}~\forall x\in[-r^{\omega},r^{\omega}].

The claimed convergence bound now follows similar to the analysis shown for Case
1
above.

Case 3. Sign transfer function:
\rho(x)=\sign(x),~\forall x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
.

In this case also,
\rho
is the
p
-th order proxy  of itself, with
p=0
,
c_{\rho}=1
and any
r\in{\mathbb{R}}
. This particular transfer function was considered in the similar optimization setup in
Saha et al. 2021b
. We show below how our proposed
Algorithm

1
(
Relative-Gradient-Descent
) generalizes their
\beta
-NGD algorithm and recovers their convergence rate of
O(\epsilon^{-1})
.

We start by noting that, our algorithm generalizes the
\beta
-NGD algorithm of
Saha et al. 2021b
.
The convergence rate claim now follows by noting that in this case our descent direction
{\mathbf{g}}_{t}
at any point
{\mathbf{w}}_{t}
, becomes the normalized gradient estimate, of
{\mathbf{w}}_{t}
with high probability (over the random draws of
{\mathbf{u}}_{t}
). Roughly speaking it can be show that

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\mid{\mathbf{w}}_{t}]\approx\frac{\tilde{c}}{\sqrt{d}}\big(\nicefrac{{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}}{{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\big),

using Lemma
2
and
4
of
Saha et al. 2021b
, or more precisely,

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\mid{\mathbf{w}}_{t}]\geq\frac{\tilde{c}}{\sqrt{d}}\bigg(\frac{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\bigg)-2\lambda\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}},

where
\lambda=\frac{3\beta\delta}{\|\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})\|}\sqrt{d\log\frac{\|\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}_{t})\|}{\sqrt{d}\beta\delta}}
.
Combining the above bound in the proof of
3
(to lower bound the term
{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\cdot({\mathbf{w}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]
), the result follows. In fact in this case the proof of
3
exactly follows the same line of argument as that of the proof of Theorem
5
of
Saha et al. 2021b
. This shows the generalization ability of our proof analysis for different special class of transfer functions.
∎

Appendix D
Appendix for
Section

4.2

D.1
Proof of
7

See
7

Complete Proof of
7
.

The proof relies on analyzing the epochwise performance guarantee of any representative run
Relative-Gradient-Descent
\big({\mathbf{w}}_{k},\eta_{k},\gamma_{k},t_{k}\big)
(see Line #5 of
Algorithm

2
).

For simplicity of notations, for any epoch
k
, inside the call of
Relative-Gradient-Descent
\big({\mathbf{w}}_{k},\eta_{k},\gamma_{k},t_{k}\big)
, let us assume
{\mathbf{x}}_{1}(={\mathbf{w}}_{k})
denotes the initial point in the run of
Relative-Gradient-Descent
(
Algorithm

1
) and let is denote by
D_{0}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
. The goal is to analyze the guarantees on the output point
{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}
of the run of
p^{th}
-degree-scaled
Gradient
after
T=t_{k}
time steps; thus
{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}={\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}
. We use the same notations as used in the proof of
3
.

Recall from
Section

C.1
, at any time step
t
inside the run of
p^{th}
-degree-scaled
Gradient
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\eta^{2},

(19)

and on the other hand, from
Section

C.1
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]={\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{d}{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\rho^{\prime}\big(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})+\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\big)\mid{\mathcal{H}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}).

(20)

Now since
f
is convex and
\beta
-smooth, applying
19
, we know that for any
{\mathbf{w}}\in{\mathcal{D}}
,

\displaystyle\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})\big)^{\top}({\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\frac{\alpha\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{w}})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

Moreover since
{\mathbf{w}}^{*}
is the minimizer of
f
in
{\mathcal{D}}
, we have
\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq 0
(from first order optimality conditions, see
14
).
Hence from above we further get:

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\big(\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})\big)\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\alpha\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

Likewise,

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})\geq\frac{\alpha\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\alpha+\beta}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

Combining the two, we get:

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})+\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\alpha\beta}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}\frac{\alpha\beta}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2})+\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\alpha\beta+\alpha)}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\gamma^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2})=\frac{2(\alpha\beta+\alpha)}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\gamma^{2}),

where the inequality
(a)
follows due to
18
. Thus we can write:

\displaystyle\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+

\displaystyle\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})+\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2(\alpha\beta+\alpha)}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\gamma^{2})-\gamma(\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t}))\boldsymbol{\cdot}{\mathbf{u}}_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}\frac{2(\alpha\beta+\alpha)}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\gamma^{2})-\gamma\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-\nabla f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2(\alpha\beta+\alpha)}{\alpha+\beta}(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+\gamma^{2})-2\beta\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-\frac{2(\beta-\tilde{\kappa})}{\tilde{\kappa}+1}\gamma^{2},

(21)

where
(b)
follows from Cauchy-Schwarz, the last inequality holds due to
16
, and
\tilde{\kappa}:=\frac{\alpha}{\beta}
.

Recall we assumed
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=D_{0}
. Let
T(=t_{k})
be the sample complexity of the run
Relative-Gradient-Descent
\big({\mathbf{w}}_{k},\eta_{k},\gamma_{k},t_{k}\big)
, i.e. the
Relative-Gradient-Descent
runs for
T
time-steps starting from the initial point
{\mathbf{x}}_{1}={\mathbf{w}}_{k}
.

Case analysis 1: (Assume
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq D_{0}/2
for all
t=[T]
).
In this case, by assumption,
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq D_{0}/2
. So from above we further get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}]\geq\frac{2\tilde{c}\alpha\gamma D_{0}}{2\sqrt{d}}-\beta\gamma^{2}.

And now note that setting
\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\alpha D_{0}}{2\beta\sqrt{d}}
, the right hand side is positive.
Further using
1
-(3) and by the definition of
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}
, we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\rho^{\prime}(\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}})]

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}(\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}})]

\displaystyle\geq{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}({\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}[\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lvert{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\right\rvert}}])

\displaystyle\geq{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\bigg(\frac{2\tilde{c}\alpha\gamma D_{0}}{2\sqrt{d}}-\beta\gamma^{2}\bigg)={\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}^{2}\alpha^{2}D_{0}^{2}}{4\beta d}\bigg).

(22)

where the second and the third inequalities are respectively due to Jensen’s inequality, as by definition
{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}
is convex (for any
p\geq 1
) in the positive orthant and also monotonically increasing.
Further by denoting
A=\frac{\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}
and
A^{\prime}=\frac{(\beta-\tilde{\kappa})}{\tilde{\kappa}+1}
, note that for this choice of
\gamma
we have

\displaystyle A\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-A^{\prime}\gamma^{2}

\displaystyle>\frac{\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}-\frac{(\beta-\tilde{\kappa})}{\tilde{\kappa}+1}\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}\alpha D_{0}}{2\beta\sqrt{d}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}\frac{\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}-\frac{(\beta-\tilde{\kappa})}{\tilde{\kappa}+1}\bigg(\frac{\alpha^{2}D_{0}^{2}}{4\beta^{2}{d}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{}{\geq}\frac{\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta{d}(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\alpha(\beta+1)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta{d}(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha(\beta+1-\alpha/d)}{\beta(\tilde{\kappa}+1)}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}>\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4},

where
(c)
follows since
\tilde{c}\leq 1
(as follows form
8
), and the last inequality is due to the fact that by definition
\beta\geq\alpha
and
d\geq 1
. Then combining above with
Sections

D.1
and
D.1
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle[{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})]\geq\frac{2\gamma}{d}{\tilde{\rho}_{p}}^{\prime}\big({\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathbf{u}}_{t}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}+\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})-f({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-\gamma{\mathbf{u}}_{t})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\big)\bigg(\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{D_{0}^{2}}{4}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\alpha D_{0}^{2}}{d(\alpha+\beta)4}\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}\alpha D_{0}}{2\beta\sqrt{d}}\bigg)\rho^{\prime}\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}^{2}\alpha^{2}D_{0}^{2}}{4\beta d}\bigg)~~(\text{from }\eqref{eq:may15},\text{ where recall we needed to set }\gamma=\frac{\tilde{c}\alpha D_{0}}{2\beta\sqrt{d}})

\displaystyle=\frac{2\alpha D_{0}^{2}}{d(\alpha+\beta)4}\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}\alpha D_{0}}{2\beta\sqrt{d}}\bigg)c_{\rho}p\bigg(\frac{\tilde{c}^{2}\alpha^{2}D_{0}^{2}}{4\beta d}\bigg)^{p-1}~~(\text{as, }\rho^{\prime}(x)=c_{\rho}px^{p-1}\text{ for any }x\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+})

\displaystyle=BD_{0}^{2p+1},~~\bigg(\text{where we denote by, }B:=\frac{2c_{\rho}p}{(\alpha+\beta)}\Big(\big(\nicefrac{{\alpha^{2}}}{{4\beta}}\big)^{p}\frac{\tilde{c}^{2p-1}}{d^{\frac{2p+1}{2}}}\Big)\bigg).

(23)

Plugging the above expression in
Equation

19
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}-2\eta{\mathbf{E}}_{t}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\cdot}({\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}]+\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D_{0}^{2}-\eta B(D_{0}^{2p+1})+\eta^{2}~~\bigg(\text{ where recall }B:=\frac{2c_{\rho}p}{(\alpha+\beta)}\Big(\big(\nicefrac{{\alpha^{2}}}{{4\beta}}\big)^{p}\frac{\tilde{c}^{2p-1}}{d^{\frac{2p+1}{2}}}\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=D_{0}^{2}-B^{2}{D_{0}^{2}}^{(2p+1)},~~\bigg(\text{ by setting }\eta=BD_{0}^{2p+1}=\frac{2c_{\rho}p}{(\alpha+\beta)}\Big(\big(\nicefrac{{\alpha^{2}}}{{4\beta}}\big)^{p}\frac{\tilde{c}^{2p-1}}{d^{\frac{2p+1}{2}}}\Big)D_{0}^{2p+1}\bigg).

(24)

Now let us fix some time-stamp
T
and let us assume
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq D_{0}/2
for all
t=[T]
.
Then taking expectation over
{\mathcal{H}}_{T+1}
on both sides iteratively and summing over
t=1,\ldots,T
, note that we get:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T+1}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq D_{0}^{2}-B^{2}{D_{0}^{2}}^{(2p+1)}T.

Then if we set
T=\frac{1}{2B^{2}{D_{0}^{2}}^{2p}}
, at time
T+1
we have:

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T+1}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq D_{0}^{2}/2<3D_{0}^{2}/4.

Case analysis 2: (
\exists
at least an
\tau\in[T]
such that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{\tau}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq D_{0}/2
).

In this case, from
Equation

19
, after
(T-\tau)
steps we can have the expected value of
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}
can be at most:

{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{T+1}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{T+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{x}}_{\tau}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}+(T-\tau)\eta^{2}\leq D_{0}^{2}/4+T\eta^{2}=3D_{0}^{2}/4,

since recall that we set
\eta=BD_{0}^{2p+1}=\frac{2c_{\rho}p}{(\alpha+\beta)}\Big(\big(\nicefrac{{\alpha^{2}}}{{4\beta}}\big)^{p}\frac{\tilde{c}^{2p-1}}{d^{\frac{2p+1}{2}}}\Big)D_{0}^{2p+1}
.

This implies that for the above choice of
\gamma
and
\eta
we can get constant fraction reduction in the “sub-optimality gap”
(\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{k}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}})
after at most
O(\frac{1}{2B^{2}{D_{0}^{2}}^{2p}})
time steps.
∎

D.2
Proof of
6

See
6

Complete Proof of
6
.

The proof of
6
is based on the key claim of
7
which shows that after every epoch of length
t_{k}
, the distance of the resulting point
{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}
from the optimal
{\mathbf{w}}^{*}
must decrease by at least a constant fraction. Recall from the statement of
7
, we have:

See
7

The claim of
6
now follows form the following
epoch-wise recursion argument:

Let
{\mathcal{H}}_{[k]}:=\{({\mathbf{w}}_{k^{\prime}})_{k^{\prime}\in[k_{\epsilon}]},({\mathbf{u}}_{t^{\prime}},o_{t^{\prime}})_{t^{\prime}\in[\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{k}t_{k^{\prime}}]}\}\cup\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}\}
denotes the complete history till the end of epoch
k
starting from the first epoch
\forall k\in[k_{\epsilon}]
.

Further, let us denote by
{\mathcal{H}}_{k}:=\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k},({\mathbf{u}}_{t^{\prime}},o_{t^{\prime}})_{t^{\prime}\in[\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{k-1}t_{k^{\prime}}+1,\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{k}t_{k^{\prime}}]}\}\cup\{{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}\}
be the history only within epoch
k
.

Proof of Correctness.
From
7
, note we have already established that

E_{{\mathcal{H}}_{k}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\mid{\mathbf{w}}_{k}]\leq{\frac{3}{4}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{k}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}
.

Applying the argument iteratively over
E
epochs, and the law of iterated expectations, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{E}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{[E]}}[\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{E+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}]\leq(\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}.

(25)

Thus choosing
E=\lceil\log_{4/3}(\frac{\beta D^{2}}{2\epsilon})\rceil
, where
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq D
,
we have

\displaystyle E\geq\log_{4/3}(\frac{\beta\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}}{2\epsilon})~\implies~(\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}})^{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathbf{w}}_{1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{*}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{\beta}.

Thus from (
25
), we get:
